00060: def finite_vector(v):
00061:     return all(math.isfinite(float(n)) for n in v)
00062: 
00063: def attach(parent, name, node):
00064:     assert name not in parent.members, f'Duplicate include key: {name}'
00065:     return parent.include(name, node)
00066: 
00067: def put(parent, name, size, center, color):
00068:     assert finite_vector(size) and all(n > 0 for n in size)
00069:     assert finite_vector(center)
00070:     return attach(parent, name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00071: 
00072: def tube(parent, name, a, b, radius=.022, color=IRON):
00073:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00074:     assert finite_vector(a) and finite_vector(b)
00075:     assert sum((a[i] - b[i]) ** 2 for i in range(3)) > 1e-12
00076:     return attach(parent, name, rod(radius=radius, start=a, end=b, color=color))
00077: 
00078: def sphere(parent, name, radius, center, color):
00079:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and finite_vector(center)
00080:     return attach(parent, name, ball(radius=radius, center=center, color=color))

00063: def finite_vector(v):
00064:     return all(math.isfinite(float(n)) for n in v)
00065: 
00066: def attach(parent, name, node):
00067:     assert name not in parent.members, f'Duplicate include key: {name}'
00068:     return parent.include(name, node)
00069: 
00070: def put(parent, name, size, center, color):
00071:     assert finite_vector(size) and all(n > 0 for n in size)
00072:     assert finite_vector(center)
00073:     return attach(parent, name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00074: 
00075: def tube(parent, name, a, b, radius=.022, color=IRON):
00076:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00077:     assert finite_vector(a) and finite_vector(b)
00078:     assert sum((a[i] - b[i]) ** 2 for i in range(3)) > 1e-12
00079:     return attach(parent, name, rod(radius=radius, start=a, end=b, color=color))
00080: 
00081: def sphere(parent, name, radius, center, color):
00082:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and finite_vector(center)
00083:     return attach(parent, name, ball(radius=radius, center=center, color=color))

00348: def isolated_materials(parent_name):
00349:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00350:     assert parent is not None, f'Missing parent: {parent_name}'
00351:     meshes = [obj for obj in parent.children_recursive if obj.type == 'MESH']
00352:     assert meshes, f'Empty mesh subtree: {parent_name}'
00353:     result = []
00354:     for obj in meshes:
00355:         assert len(obj.material_slots) > 0
00356:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00357:             assert slot.material is not None
00358:             slot.material = slot.material.copy()
00359:             slot.material.use_nodes = True
00360:             bsdf = slot.material.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00361:             assert bsdf is not None
00362:             result.append(bsdf)
00363:     return result
00364:

00369:             emission = bsdf.inputs.get('Emission')

00052: def positive(v):
00053:     assert math.isfinite(v) and v > 0
00054:     return v
00055: 
00056: 
00057: def point(p):
00058:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00059:     return tuple(p)
00060: 
00061: 
00062: def attach(parent, key, node):
00063:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00064:     return parent.include(key, node)
00065: 
00066: 
00067: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color, radius=None):
00068:     size = tuple(positive(v) for v in size)
00069:     center = point(center)
00070:     if radius is None:
00071:         node = box(size, center=center, color=color)
00072:     else:
00073:         positive(radius)
00074:         assert radius <= min(size) / 2, (key, size, radius)
00075:         node = fillet_box(size, radius, center=center, color=color)
00076:     return attach(parent, key, node)
00077: 
00078: 
00079: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00080:     positive(radius)
00081:     points = [point(p) for p in points]
00082:     assert len(points) >= 2
00083:     assert all(sum((a[k] - b[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00084:                for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])), key
00085:     return attach(parent, key,
00086:                   sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00087: 
00088: 
00089: def cylinder(parent, key, radius, height, center, color):
00090:     positive(radius)
00091:     positive(height)
00092:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, length=height,
00093:                                   center=point(center), color=color))
00094: 
00095: 
00096: def segment(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00097:     positive(radius)
00098:     start, end = point(start), point(end)
00099:     assert sum((start[k] - end[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00100:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00101: 
00102: 
00103: def sphere(parent, key, radius, center, color):
00104:     positive(radius)
00105:     return attach(parent, key, ball(radius, center=point(center), color=color))

00316: def meshes_under(label):
00317:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(label)
00318:     assert parent is not None, label
00319:     result = []
00320: 
00321:     def walk(obj):
00322:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00323:             result.append(obj)
00324:         for child in obj.children:
00325:             walk(child)
00326: 
00327:     walk(parent)
00328:     assert result, label
00329:     return result
00330:

00340:     return result

00045: 
00046: 
00047: def point(value):
00048:     assert len(value) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in value)
00049:     return tuple(value)
00050: 
00051: 
00052: def attach(parent, key, node):
00053:     assert key not in parent.members, f'Duplicate child key: {key}'
00054:     return parent.include(key, node)
00055: 
00056: 
00057: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color, radius=None):
00058:     size = tuple(positive(v) for v in size)
00059:     center = point(center)
00060:     if radius is None:
00061:         node = box(size, center=center, color=color)
00062:     else:
00063:         positive(radius)
00064:         assert radius <= min(size) / 2, (key, size, radius)
00065:         node = fillet_box(size, radius, center=center, color=color)
00066:     return attach(parent, key, node)
00067: 
00068: 
00069: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00070:     positive(radius)
00071:     points = [point(p) for p in points]
00072:     assert len(points) >= 2
00073:     assert all(
00074:         sum((a[k] - b[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00075:         for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])
00076:     ), key
00077:     return attach(
00078:         parent, key,
00079:         sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00080:     )
00081: 
00082: 
00083: def cylinder(parent, key, radius, height, center, color):
00084:     positive(radius)
00085:     positive(height)
00086:     return attach(
00087:         parent, key,
00088:         rod(radius, length=height, center=point(center), color=color)
00089:     )
00090: 
00091: 
00092: def segment(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00093:     positive(radius)
00094:     start, end = point(start), point(end)
00095:     assert sum((start[k] - end[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16, key
00096:     return attach(
00097:         parent, key,
00098:         rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00099:     )
00100: 
00101: 
00102: def sphere(parent, key, radius, center, color):
00103:     positive(radius)
00104:     return attach(
00105:         parent, key, ball(radius, center=point(center), color=color)
00106:     )

00332: 
00333: def meshes_under(label):
00334:     """Resolve a named SceneAct parent, including all descendant meshes."""
00335:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(label)
00336:     assert parent is not None, f'Missing named parent: {label}'
00337:     result = []
00338: 
00339:     def walk(obj):
00340:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00341:             result.append(obj)
00342:         for child in obj.children:
00343:             walk(child)
00344: 
00345:     walk(parent)
00346:     assert result, f'No meshes below named parent: {label}'
00347:     return result
00348: 
00349: 
00350: def isolated_materials(obj):
00351:     """Copy each shared slot before making any local shader edits."""
00352:     result = []
00353:     for slot in obj.material_slots:
00354:         if slot.material is not None:
00355:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00356:             slot.material = mat
00357:             mat.use_nodes = True
00358:             result.append(mat)
00359:     return result
00360:

00046: def positive(v):
00047:     assert math.isfinite(v) and v > 0
00048:     return v
00049: 
00050: 
00051: def point(p):
00052:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00053:     return tuple(p)
00054: 
00055: 
00056: def attach(parent, key, node):
00057:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00058:     return parent.include(key, node)
00059: 
00060: 
00061: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color, radius=None):
00062:     size = tuple(positive(v) for v in size)
00063:     center = point(center)
00064:     if radius is None:
00065:         node = box(size, center=center, color=color)
00066:     else:
00067:         positive(radius)
00068:         assert radius <= min(size) / 2, (key, size, radius)
00069:         node = fillet_box(size, radius, center=center, color=color)
00070:     return attach(parent, key, node)
00071: 
00072: 
00073: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00074:     positive(radius)
00075:     points = [point(p) for p in points]
00076:     assert len(points) >= 2
00077:     assert all(sum((a[k]-b[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00078:                for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])), key
00079:     return attach(parent, key,
00080:                   sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00081: 
00082: 
00083: def cylinder(parent, key, radius, height, center, color):
00084:     positive(radius)
00085:     positive(height)
00086:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, length=height,
00087:                                   center=point(center), color=color))
00088: 
00089: 
00090: def segment(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00091:     positive(radius)
00092:     start, end = point(start), point(end)
00093:     assert sum((start[k]-end[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00094:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00095: 
00096: 
00097: def sphere(parent, key, radius, center, color):
00098:     positive(radius)
00099:     return attach(parent, key, ball(radius, center=point(center), color=color))

00287: def meshes_under(label):
00288:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(label)
00289:     assert parent is not None, label
00290:     result = []
00291:     def walk(obj):
00292:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00293:             result.append(obj)
00294:         for child in obj.children:
00295:             walk(child)
00296:     walk(parent)
00297:     assert result, label
00298:     return result
00299: 
00300: 
00301: def isolated_materials(obj):
00302:     result = []
00303:     for slot in obj.material_slots:
00304:         if slot.material is not None:
00305:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00306:             slot.material = mat
00307:             mat.use_nodes = True
00308:             result.append(mat)
00309:     return result
